<script type="text/javascript">
											$(document).ready(function() {
										        
												$('#search_query').autocomplete({
													delay: 0,
													appendTo: "#autocomplete-results",
													source: function(request, response) {	
										        
										                var category_filter = $('header select[name=\'category_id\']').val();
										                var category_filter_url = '';
										                if (category_filter) {
										                    category_filter_url = '&filter_category_id=' + encodeURIComponent(category_filter);
										                }
														$.ajax({
															url: 'index.php?route=search/autocomplete&filter_name=' +  encodeURIComponent(request.term) + category_filter_url,
															dataType: 'json',
															success: function(json) {
																response($.map(json, function(item) {
																	return {
																		label: item.name,
																		value: item.product_id,
																		href: item.href,
																		thumb: item.thumb,
																		desc: item.desc,
																		price: item.price,
																		special: item.special

																	}
																}));
															}
														});
													},
													select: function(event, ui) {
														document.location.href = ui.item.href;
														
														return false;
													},
													focus: function(event, ui) {
												      	return false;
												   	},
												   	minLength: 2
												})

												.data( "ui-autocomplete" )._renderItem = function( ul, item ) {
													if (item.special == false) {
													return $( "<li>" )
												    .append( "<a><img src='" + item.thumb + "' alt=''>" + item.label + "<br><span class='price'>" + item.price + "</span></a>" )
												    .appendTo( ul );
													} else {
													return $( "<li>" )
												    .append( "<a><img src='" + item.thumb + "' alt=''>" + item.label + "<br><span style='text-decoration: line-through' class='price-old'>" + item.price + "</span><span class='price'>" + item.special + "</span></a>" )
												    .appendTo( ul );
													}
												};
											});
											</script>

<h4>Новостная рассылка</h4><div class="newsletter clearfix" id="newsletter30217752"><p>Получите всю последнюю информацию о событиях, продажах и предложениях. Подпишитесь на рассылку новостей сегодня.</p><div class="inputs"><input class="email" placeholder="Email адрес" type="text"/><a class="button subscribe">Подписаться</a></div></div>
<script type="text/javascript">
$(document).ready(function() {
	function Unsubscribe() {
		$.post('https://myshops.ae/index.php?route=extension/module/newsletter/unsubscribe', 
			{ 
				email: $('#newsletter30217752 .email').val() 
			}, function (e) {
				$('#newsletter30217752 .email').val('');
				alert(e.message);
			}
		, 'json');
	}
	
	function Subscribe() {
		$.post('https://myshops.ae/index.php?route=extension/module/newsletter/subscribe', 
			{ 
				email: $('#newsletter30217752 .email').val() 
			}, function (e) {
				if(e.error === 1) {
					var r = confirm(e.message);
					if (r == true) {
					    $.post('https://myshops.ae/index.php?route=extension/module/newsletter/unsubscribe', { 
					    	email: $('#newsletter30217752 .email').val() 
					    }, function (e) {
					    	$('#newsletter30217752 .email').val('');
					    	alert(e.message);
					    }, 'json');
					}
				} else {
					$('#newsletter30217752 .email').val('');
					alert(e.message);
				}
			}
		, 'json');
	}
	
	$('#newsletter30217752 .subscribe').click(Subscribe);
	$('#newsletter30217752 .unsubscribe').click(Unsubscribe);
	$('#newsletter30217752 .email').keypress(function (e) {
	    if (e.which == 13) {
	        Subscribe();
	    }
	});
});
</script>

	$('.colorboxbtn').on('click',function(){
		var product_option_value_id = $(this).attr('rel');
		var product_id = $(this).attr('rel2');
		 $.ajax({
			url: 'get_image_by_color.php?product_id='+product_id+'&product_option_value_id='+product_option_value_id,
			type: 'get',                 
			success: function(html) {
					$('.replace_image_'+product_id).attr('src', 'http://myshops.uzeyirxt.beget.tech/image/' + html);
			},
			error: function(xhr, ajaxOptions, thrownError) {
				// alert(thrownError + "\r\n" + xhr.statusText + "\r\n" + xhr.responseText);
			}
		 });
	});
</script>
